tbmm-2019-01-09sit01spe0002par0015	Mr. President, dear MPs, I salute the General Assembly. The main goal in fighting bio-smuggling is to prevent all or part of all plants or animals or fossils, which are our national assets within our political borders, from being smuggled out of the country illegally. The Ministry of Agriculture and Forestry officially reflects on the figures where trafficking has reached as part of a project launched in 81 districts on bio-smuggling in 2013, and has become a subject of newspapers, television and press. In the mosques on this subject, bio-smuggling was reported in the news, the law enforcement forces were briefed, our security forces were conscious and involved. The struggle continues in our country. As part of this struggle, the Ministry of Agriculture should actually go to co-operation with the Ministry of National Education and raise awareness of our students and young people involved in bio-smuggling in schools. These applications have had negative effects on local researchers. Native researchers are particularly difficult in terms of time and bureaucracy, but not to be forgotten, plants out of the country have diverse feet, and one of them is international joint projects. It's in the official record of bio-smuggling with scientific studies, which seem to be very innocent. Therefore, the Ministry of Agriculture has stipulated that local researchers can only collect plants by permission and that a copy of the work is given to the relevant Ministry. Most registered species come from foreign countries in our country. Although our country is an important plant centre, the TAGAM, universities and private sector rehabilitation agencies do not have the potential to acquire enough new species. This situation must be studied very well. If we can't use that biological potential, we'll be dependent on the outside and unable to produce seeds in the future. Fluoristics in Turkey dates back to 1,700. After the 1600s, the botanists began to grow in our country, but when we look at scientists doing research in our country, we see that they are British, French, Dutch, Swiss and German. When we look at their professions, when we look at where they work, when we look at the groups they work in, there are a lot of different things. One of them, again, is that bio-smuggling exists, and we find that some of their professions are doctors, pharmacists, agriculturalists, agents, and some of them are flower traders. Abducted animals are medical aromatic plants, industrial plants, spices and ornamental plants. Besides, Hopa vipers, bumblebees, Kangal dogs, are also among the organisms or creatures that have been abducted. In international projects, especially in our country's industrial animals and plants, they export genes to get new culture plants, gain a significant amount of income by selling them to our country at high prices again, by getting cold, heat, drought and disease-resistant species. At the same time, they possess genes found in plants that don't grow in their ecology. To prevent this, it is necessary to remove about 3,000 endemic plants and animal DNA bands in our country and register genes on behalf of our country, and to study the projects done by the university, private sector and TAGEM in international projects very well by experts.

The 14th, 15th and 16th articles of the proposal have made arrangements to change the name of İstanbul University of Science to the University of Demiroğlu, Gaziantep University of Science and Technology, and the name of Gaziantep Islamic University of Science and Technology. Under Article 17 of the proposal, changes are made in the scope of the proposal, since the office and authority of the Secretary General of the National Security Council is involved in the law as well as in the presidential decree. Article 18 of the proposal includes the delivery and delivery of books and long-term publications provided by publishers of the Ministry of Culture and Tourism with the surrender of organised industrial regions and other energy facilities, excluding the value-appropriation tax. The 19th article of the proposal is to include the differences in foreign money-based surrender and services within the country in the value-worth tax matrix, with which hesitations are intended to be resolved. Article 20 of the proposal is extended to 31 December 2020, considering that the transfer of the transports to the treasury as part of urban transformation projects will not be profitable due to the transfer of the transports to the treasury, and the execution period of the provisions, which will be excluded from the value-appropriation tax of the same transactions and surrenders, is incomplete with the Sultanbeyli municipality. The 21st article of the proposal is authorised in 2019 to continue implementing value-appropriation tax and institutional tax incentives for investments in the manufacturing industry, which can be extended for up to five years. In Articles 22 and 23 of the proposal, international good practices are also taken into account as part of the country's needs, and the Financial Stabilisation Committee is being restructured under the name "Finance Stabilisation and Development Committee" and its mandate definitions. Under the 24th article of the proposal, areas used as mera, pedestrians and barracks are possible to change the purpose of the allocation of areas which are essential for geothermal resources and natural mineral waters. This law wishes that our offer be good for our country and our nation, and I salute our Veteran Assembly and the great Turkish nation watching us on screen.

Urban transformation represents social dynamics, and it is a multi-sponsored structure consisting of central and local governments, investors and citizens. Therefore, if the urban transformation cannot be done with a comprehensive and integrated vision and action, unfortunately, it cannot achieve its goal. And when we look at the history of urban transformation in our country, unfortunately, we don't see a mission or vision. Although urban transformation is a system that has been created to create sustainable and modern cities, it's unfortunate that when it's ruled by the wrong people and for different purposes, it's difficult to return to the structure of cities. There is a 73 per cent difference between the Ministry of Environment and Urbanism's Risk Zone Report and the risk map published by the Japanese International Co-operation Agency in our country. Could it be that big a difference? The Transformation of Areas Under the Disaster No. 6306: This law, which is called the urban transformation, is aimed at creating community residential centres with its infrastructure, which, as you can see from its name, will not break the integrity of the city, instead of the earthquake-resistant structures that have been destroyed and transformed into urban transformations. Instead of bringing down unrestrained structures and replacing them with practical and aesthetics, habitable structures, instead of opening the way for horizontal urbanization, skyscrapers were built vertically. Hence, the culture of life in certain areas has also been corrupted. We turned Turkey into a big construction site." Because they perceived urban transformation as just the transformation of physical structures, they never considered the socioeconomic dimensions of this. A total of 18 months of rent allowances have been down to two months, I'm afraid. Without control, the citizen gets hit.
